> 91	function reframeChain(ar: number, W: number, H: number): string {

> 163	/** Segment a master into N clips per the cut-spec. Serial (deterministic, memory-safe for a long master). */
> 164	export async function segmentVideo(spec: SegmentSpec): Promise<ClipResult[]> {
> 165	  if (!existsSync(spec.sourcePath)) throw new Error(`source_not_found: ${spec.sourcePath}`);
> 166	  if (!Array.isArray(spec.clips) || spec.clips.length === 0) throw new Error('cut_spec_empty');
> 167	  mkdirSync(spec.outDir, { recursive: true });
> 168	  const out: ClipResult[] = [];
> 169	  for (let i = 0; i < spec.clips.length; i++) {
> 170	    const c = spec.clips[i];
> 171	    if (!(c.out_sec > c.in_sec) || c.in_sec < 0) throw new Error(`clip_${i}_invalid_range`);
> 172	    out.push(await renderClip(spec, c, i));
> 173	  }
> 174	  return out;
> 175	}

> ```
> 1	#!/usr/bin/env bun
> 2	/**
> 3	 * scripts/video-repurpose/zone-saliency.ts
> 4	 *
> 5	 * Zone-saliency picker for the motion-graphics caption family (Fatia 1, Opção B, US$0). Given a master and a
> 6	 * cut window, it chooses the CALMEST corner/edge zone of the reframed 9:16 frame so the 2-level motion-graphics
> 7	 * card lands in the VOID instead of over the subject. This is the "Option B" of the design decision: a
> 8	 * single-pass edge-density scorer seeded by a deterministic prior (Option A), NOT face-aware compositing
> 9	 * (Option C, deferred to Fatia 2). No LLM, no model download, no per-frame US$ — pure FFmpeg + arithmetic.
> 10	 *
> 11	 * The motion-graphics style COEXISTS with the 7 karaoke caption styles (segment-core CAPTION_STYLES); it is one
> 12	 * more member of the family, and this module only answers "where is it calmest?". Placement/rendering is the
> 13	 * consumer's job — it reads `zone`/`rect` and anchors the card there.
> 14	 *
> 15	 * ── How agitation is scored (validated recommendation, do NOT re-derive) ─────────────────────────────────
> 16	 * For up to 3 instants of the cut (start / mid / end — the WORST instant governs, so a card is never placed
> 17	 * where the footage is briefly busy), the reframed frame is split into 6 candidate zones (a 2-column ×
> 18	 * 3-row grid inside the safe area, with a wide CENTER GUTTER so the subject band is never a candidate). Each
> 19	 * zone is cropped → `edgedetect` → `signalstats`, and the mean luma of the edge map (`YAVG`) is read as an
> 20	 * edge-density proxy for AGITATION. Calmest zone = lowest max-YAVG. Measured cost (this host): one 6-zone
> 21	 * single-frame pass = ~0.20s, so a 3-instant cut ≈ 0.6s — inside the 0.3–0.9s/cut budget.
> 22	 *
> 23	 * ── Fail-open (cosmetic, never load-bearing) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 24	 * Zone choice is COSMETIC. If FFmpeg fails, the source is missing, or a zone yields no reading, this returns
> 25	 * the deterministic default `lower-left` with `degraded:true` — it NEVER throws and NEVER blocks a render. A
> 26	 * card in a slightly busier corner is a far cheaper failure than a dropped multi-minute re-encode.
> 27	 *
> 28	 * SECURITY: every spawn takes an ARRAY of args — never a shell string (the source path is caller data). The
> 29	 * crop/reframe expressions carry no interpolated caller data (only fixed zone fractions), so they are inert.
> 30	 *
> 31	 * SOP SSOT: docs/processes/motion-graphics-zone-saliency.md (Lei 2). Design proof: proto-motiongfx flagship.
> 32	 */
> 33	
> 34	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
> 35	import { existsSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
> 36	import { join } from 'node:path';
> 37	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
> 38	
> 39	/** The 6 candidate zones — a 2-column × 3-row grid, biased to the left/right bands (no center column). */
> 40	export type Zone =
> 41	  | 'lower-left' | 'lower-right'
> 42	  | 'mid-left'   | 'mid-right'
> 43	  | 'upper-left' | 'upper-right';
> 44	
> 45	/** Fractional placement box in canvas space (0..1), same coordinate system a CSS consumer positions against. */
> 46	export interface ZoneRect {
> 47	  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
> 48	}
> 49	
> 50	/** One zone's agitation reading (mean edge-map luma, temporal MAX over the sampled instants) + its rect. */
> 51	export interface ZoneScore {
> 52	  zone: Zone;
> 53	  /** Edge-density proxy (`YAVG` of the edge-detected crop). Higher = busier footage. 0 = perfectly flat. */
> 54	  agitation: number;
> 55	  rect: ZoneRect;
> 56	}
> 57	
> 58	export interface ZonePick {
> 59	  /** The chosen (calmest, tie-broken by preference) zone enum. */
> 60	  zone: Zone;
> 61	  /** The chosen zone's fractional placement box — the consumer anchors the card inside this. */
> 62	  rect: ZoneRect;
> 63	  /** Every zone ranked calmest-first. `scores[0].zone === zone` unless `degraded`. */
> 64	  scores: ZoneScore[];
> 65	  /** True when the fail-open default was returned (FFmpeg/source failure); the pick is then not measured. */
> 66	  degraded: boolean;
> 67	  /** Measured wall time of the whole pick, ms (Lei 1 — the real cost, reported by the smoke). */
> 68	  elapsedMs: number;
> 69	}
> 70	
> 71	export interface ZoneSaliencyOptions {
> 72	  /** Analysis frame width; the frame is reframed+scaled to this before scoring. Default 540 (½ of 1080). */
> 73	  workW?: number;
> 74	  /** Analysis frame height. Default 960 (½ of 1920). Keep the 9:16 aspect. */
> 75	  workH?: number;
> 76	  /** Reframe aspect of the analysis frame (master is center-cropped to this). Default 9/16. */
> 77	  aspect?: number;
> 78	  /** Max instants sampled across the cut (start/mid/end). Default 3; clamped to 1..3. */
> 79	  sampleCount?: number;
> 80	  /** FFmpeg binary. Default 'ffmpeg'. */
> 81	  ffmpegPath?: string;
> 82	}
> 83	
> 84	// ── Zone geometry (fractional canvas coords) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 85	// Safe area: sides ~4.5%, top ~7%, bottom ~13% (leaves room for platform UI/caption). Two columns separated by
> 86	// a wide CENTER GUTTER [0.42,0.58] so the subject band is structurally excluded (the "vertical band bias"). Three
> 87	// rows inside the vertical safe area. Every cell is 0.375×0.24 of canvas (405×461px at 1080×1920) — comfortably
> 88	// above MIN_ZONE_* below, so the 2-level card always fits.
> 89	const COL_LEFT_X = 0.045;
> 90	const COL_RIGHT_X = 0.58;
> 91	const COL_W = 0.375;
> 92	const ROW_UPPER_Y = 0.07;
> 93	const ROW_MID_Y = 0.35;
> 94	const ROW_LOWER_Y = 0.63;
> 95	const ROW_H = 0.24;
> 96	
> 97	export const ZONE_RECTS: Record<Zone, ZoneRect> = {
> 98	  'upper-left':  { x: COL_LEFT_X,  y: ROW_UPPER_Y, w: COL_W, h: ROW_H },
> 99	  'upper-right': { x: COL_RIGHT_X, y: ROW_UPPER_Y, w: COL_W, h: ROW_H },
> 100	  'mid-left':    { x: COL_LEFT_X,  y: ROW_MID_Y,   w: COL_W, h: ROW_H },
> 101	  'mid-right':   { x: COL_RIGHT_X, y: ROW_MID_Y,   w: COL_W, h: ROW_H },
> 102	  'lower-left':  { x: COL_LEFT_X,  y: ROW_LOWER_Y, w: COL_W, h: ROW_H },
> 103	  'lower-right': { x: COL_RIGHT_X, y: ROW_LOWER_Y, w: COL_W, h: ROW_H },
> 104	};
> 105	
> 106	/**
> 107	 * Tie-break preference when zones are equally calm: lower band first (the conventional caption third, least
> 108	 * likely to cover a face which usually sits upper/center), then mid, then upper. This is also the fail-open
> 109	 * default order — `PREFERENCE[0]` is the safe default.
> 110	 */
> 111	export const PREFERENCE: Zone[] = [
> 112	  'lower-left', 'lower-right', 'mid-left', 'mid-right', 'upper-left', 'upper-right',
> 113	];
> 114	export const DEFAULT_ZONE: Zone = PREFERENCE[0];
> 115	
> 116	/** Min footprint the 2-level card needs; a zone below this is excluded from selection (defensive — the fixed
> 117	 * grid always clears it, but a future geometry change must not silently pick an unusable sliver). */
> 118	const MIN_ZONE_W_FRAC = 0.25;
> 119	const MIN_ZONE_H_FRAC = 0.14;
> 120	
> 121	/** YAVG band width under which two zones count as "equally calm" (edge-map luma units). Buckets the score so
> 122	 * near-identical calm regions defer to PREFERENCE instead of chasing sub-perceptual float noise. */
> 123	const CALM_EPS = 0.5;
> 124	
> 125	/** Zones ordered by tie-break preference index (for stable "calmest wins, else preferred" comparison). */
> 126	const PREF_INDEX: Record<Zone, number> = PREFERENCE.reduce(
> 127	  (acc, z, i) => { acc[z] = i; return acc; },
> 128	  {} as Record<Zone, number>,
> 129	);
> 130	
> 131	/** Look up a zone's fractional placement box. */
> 132	export function rectForZone(zone: Zone): ZoneRect {
> 133	  return ZONE_RECTS[zone];
> 134	}
> 135	
> 136	/** A zone is selectable only if its footprint clears the card minimum. */
> 137	function zoneFits(zone: Zone): boolean {
> 138	  const r = ZONE_RECTS[zone];
> 139	  return r.w >= MIN_ZONE_W_FRAC && r.h >= MIN_ZONE_H_FRAC;
> 140	}
> 141	
> 142	/** Source-agnostic center-crop to `aspect`, then scale to (W,H). Mirrors segment-core's reframe so the zones
> 143	 * align with exactly the pixels the render will overlay. Commas inside min() are escaped for filtergraph. */
> 144	function reframeChain(aspect: number, w: number, h: number): string {
> 145	  const cw = `min(iw\\,ih*${aspect})`;
> 146	  const ch = `min(ih\\,iw/${aspect})`;
> 147	  return `crop=${cw}:${ch}:(iw-ow)/2:(ih-oh)/2,scale=${w}:${h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=${w}:${h},setsar=1`;
> 148	}
> 149	
> 150	interface RunResult { code: number; stderr: string; }
> 151	
> 152	function runFfmpeg(cmd: string, args: string[]): Promise<RunResult> {
> 153	  return new Promise((resolvePromise) => {
> 154	    let p;
> 155	    try {
> 156	      p = spawn(cmd, args, { stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
> 157	    } catch (e) {
> 158	      // spawn itself threw (binary missing) — fail-open, never surface to the caller.
> 159	      resolvePromise({ code: -1, stderr: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
> 160	      return;
> 161	    }
> 162	    let stderr = '';
> 163	    p.stderr.on('data', (d: Buffer) => { stderr += d.toString(); });
> 164	    p.on('error', (e: Error) => resolvePromise({ code: -1, stderr: e.message }));
> 165	    p.on('close', (code: number | null) => resolvePromise({ code: code ?? -1, stderr }));
> 166	  });
> 167	}
> 168	
> 169	/** Order zones the render walks the grid in — kept fixed so per-zone metadata filenames are deterministic. */
> 170	const ZONE_ORDER: Zone[] = ['upper-left', 'upper-right', 'mid-left', 'mid-right', 'lower-left', 'lower-right'];
> 171	
> 172	/** Build the single-frame, 6-zone FFmpeg pass for one instant `t`. Each zone writes its YAVG to its own file. */
> 173	function buildPassArgs(
> 174	  ffmpegPath: string, sourcePath: string, t: number, workW: number, workH: number, aspect: number,
> 175	  metaDir: string, sampleIdx: number,
> 176	): { args: string[]; files: Record<Zone, string> } {
> 177	  const reframe = reframeChain(aspect, workW, workH);
> 178	  const chains: string[] = [];
> 179	  const maps: string[] = [];
> 180	  const files = {} as Record<Zone, string>;
> 181	  ZONE_ORDER.forEach((zone, i) => {
> 182	    const r = ZONE_RECTS[zone];
> 183	    const file = join(metaDir, `s${sampleIdx}_${i}.txt`);
> 184	    files[zone] = file;
> 185	    // crop uses only fixed fractions of iw/ih (no commas, no caller data) → inert in the filtergraph.
> 186	    chains.push(
> 187	      `[s${i}]crop=iw*${r.w}:ih*${r.h}:iw*${r.x}:ih*${r.y},edgedetect,signalstats,metadata=print:file=${file}[o${i}]`,
> 188	    );
> 189	    maps.push('-map', `[o${i}]`, '-f', 'null', '-');
> 190	  });
> 191	  const splitLabels = ZONE_ORDER.map((_, i) => `[s${i}]`).join('');
> 192	  // select=eq(n\,0) → exactly the one seeked frame, so each null output emits a single frame then EOFs.
> 193	  const filterComplex =
> 194	    `[0:v]${reframe},select=eq(n\\,0),split=${ZONE_ORDER.length}${splitLabels};${chains.join(';')}`;
> 195	  const args = [
> 196	    '-v', 'error',
> 197	    '-ss', Math.max(0, t).toFixed(3),
> 198	    '-i', sourcePath,
> 199	    '-filter_complex', filterComplex,
> 200	    ...maps,
> 201	  ];
> 202	  return { args, files };
> 203	}
> 204	
> 205	/** Parse the MAX YAVG in a metadata=print file (single frame → one line, but MAX is robust to more). */
> 206	function readYavg(file: string): number | null {
> 207	  if (!existsSync(file)) return null;
> 208	  let text: string;
> 209	  try { text = readFileSync(file, 'utf8'); } catch { return null; }
> 210	  let max: number | null = null;
> 211	  const re = /YAVG=([-\d.]+)/g;
> 212	  let m: RegExpExecArray | null;
> 213	  while ((m = re.exec(text)) !== null) {
> 214	    const v = Number.parseFloat(m[1]);
> 215	    if (Number.isFinite(v)) max = max === null ? v : Math.max(max, v);
> 216	  }
> 217	  return max;
> 218	}
> 219	
> 220	/** Deterministic sample instants across the cut: start / mid / end (end nudged just inside the window). */
> 221	function sampleTimes(inSec: number, outSec: number, count: number): number[] {
> 222	  const start = Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(inSec) ? inSec : 0);
> 223	  const end = Math.max(start, Number.isFinite(outSec) ? outSec : start);
> 224	  const span = end - start;
> 225	  if (span <= 0.3 || count <= 1) return [start];
> 226	  const nudge = Math.min(0.1, span * 0.05);
> 227	  const mid = start + span / 2;
> 228	  const last = Math.max(start, end - nudge);
> 229	  const all = count >= 3 ? [start, mid, last] : [start, last];
> 230	  // Dedupe near-identical instants (a tiny window can collapse mid/last onto the endpoints).
> 231	  const out: number[] = [];
> 232	  for (const t of all) if (!out.some((u) => Math.abs(u - t) < 0.02)) out.push(t);
> 233	  return out;
> 234	}
> 235	
> 236	/** The fail-open default result — deterministic, unmeasured, never throws. */
> 237	function defaultPick(elapsedMs: number): ZonePick {
> 238	  const scores: ZoneScore[] = PREFERENCE.map((zone) => ({ zone, agitation: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, rect: ZONE_RECTS[zone] }));
> 239	  return { zone: DEFAULT_ZONE, rect: ZONE_RECTS[DEFAULT_ZONE], scores, degraded: true, elapsedMs };
> 240	}
> 241	
> 242	/**
> 243	 * Pick the calmest zone for the motion-graphics card over the cut window [in_sec, out_sec].
> 244	 *
> 245	 * Fail-open contract: NEVER throws. On any FFmpeg/source failure (or insufficient readings) it returns the
> 246	 * deterministic `lower-left` default with `degraded:true`. Zone choice is cosmetic — it must never break a render.
> 247	 */
> 248	export async function pickCalmestZone(
> 249	  sourcePath: string,
> 250	  inSec: number,
> 251	  outSec: number,
> 252	  opts: ZoneSaliencyOptions = {},
> 253	): Promise<ZonePick> {
> 254	  const t0 = performance.now();
> 255	  const workW = Math.max(2, Math.floor(opts.workW ?? 540));
> 256	  const workH = Math.max(2, Math.floor(opts.workH ?? 960));
> 257	  const aspect = opts.aspect && opts.aspect > 0 ? opts.aspect : 9 / 16;
> 258	  const sampleCount = Math.min(3, Math.max(1, Math.floor(opts.sampleCount ?? 3)));
> 259	  const ffmpegPath = opts.ffmpegPath ?? 'ffmpeg';
> 260	
> 261	  if (!existsSync(sourcePath)) return defaultPick(performance.now() - t0);
> 262	
> 263	  const times = sampleTimes(inSec, outSec, sampleCount);
> 264	  // Per-zone temporal MAX of agitation — the worst instant governs so the card never lands where the footage
> 265	  // is briefly busy. Undefined until a zone gets at least one reading.
> 266	  const maxAgitation = {} as Partial<Record<Zone, number>>;
> 267	  let metaDir: string | null = null;
> 268	  try {
> 269	    metaDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'zone-saliency-'));
> 270	    for (let s = 0; s < times.length; s++) {
> 271	      const { args, files } = buildPassArgs(ffmpegPath, sourcePath, times[s], workW, workH, aspect, metaDir, s);
> 272	      const res = await runFfmpeg(ffmpegPath, args);
> 273	      if (res.code !== 0) continue; // fail-soft per instant; other instants may still succeed
> 274	      for (const zone of ZONE_ORDER) {
> 275	        const v = readYavg(files[zone]);
> 276	        if (v === null) continue;
> 277	        const prev = maxAgitation[zone];
> 278	        maxAgitation[zone] = prev === undefined ? v : Math.max(prev, v);
> 279	      }
> 280	    }
> 281	  } catch {
> 282	    return defaultPick(performance.now() - t0);
> 283	  } finally {
> 284	    if (metaDir) rmSync(metaDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
> 285	  }
> 286	
> 287	  // Only zones that (a) got a reading and (b) fit the card minimum are selectable.
> 288	  const selectable = ZONE_ORDER.filter((z) => maxAgitation[z] !== undefined && zoneFits(z));
> 289	  if (selectable.length === 0) return defaultPick(performance.now() - t0);
> 290	
> 291	  const scores: ZoneScore[] = selectable
> 292	    .map((zone) => ({ zone, agitation: maxAgitation[zone] as number, rect: ZONE_RECTS[zone] }))
> 293	    // Calmest first: bucket agitation by CALM_EPS so near-equal calm zones defer to PREFERENCE (transitive).
> 294	    .sort((a, b) => {
> 295	      const ba = Math.round(a.agitation / CALM_EPS);
> 296	      const bb = Math.round(b.agitation / CALM_EPS);
> 297	      if (ba !== bb) return ba - bb;
> 298	      return PREF_INDEX[a.zone] - PREF_INDEX[b.zone];
> 299	    });
> 300	
> 301	  const chosen = scores[0].zone;
> 302	  return {
> 303	    zone: chosen,
> 304	    rect: ZONE_RECTS[chosen],
> 305	    scores,
> 306	    degraded: false,
> 307	    elapsedMs: performance.now() - t0,
> 308	  };
> 309	}
> 310	
> ```

> 51	// FR-VR-011: validate punctual motion-graphic beats against the clip window; drop malformed (never fabricate).

> 94	        action: `O corte #${i + 1} pede legendas em "beats" mas nenhuma frase válida sobrou (fora da janela do corte ou sem texto). Rode a detecção de momentos novamente ou escolha outro modo de legenda.`,
> 95	      };
> 96	    }
> 97	    // Caption style is COSMETIC, so an unknown slug COLLAPSES to the default instead of 422-ing the job.
> 98	    // Rejecting a whole multi-clip render — the expensive part — over a decorative field would be
> 99	    // disproportionate: the caller loses minutes of work and gets no video, when the honest outcome is
> 100	    // "you got captions, just not the skin you asked for". Contrast caption_mode='beats' with no valid
> 101	    // beats above, which DOES fail closed — there the defect costs the user a NUDE video (no captions at
> 102	    // all), so silence would be the harmful choice. Only carried for the beats path; drawtext burns its
> 103	    // own bar and has no template.
> 104	    const caption_style = caption_mode === "beats"
> 105	      ? (CAPTION_STYLES.has(String((c as ClipIn).caption_style)) ? String((c as ClipIn).caption_style) : DEFAULT_CAPTION_STYLE)
> 106	      : undefined;
> 107	    clips.push({ in_sec: inS, out_sec: outS, caption, reframe, caption_mode, text_beats, caption_style });
> 108	  }
> 109	  return { clips };
> 110	}
> 111	
> 112	function sanitizeSlides(raw: unknown): { slides: SlideIn[]; error?: string } {
> 113	  if (!Array.isArray(raw) || raw.length === 0) return { slides: [], error: "slide_spec_empty" };
> 114	  if (raw.length > MAX_SLIDES) return { slides: [], error: "too_many_slides" };
> 115	  const slides: SlideIn[] = [];
> 116	  for (const s of raw) {
> 117	    const t = Number((s as SlideIn)?.t_sec);
> 118	    if (!Number.isFinite(t) || t < 0) return { slides: [], error: "slide_time_invalid" };
> 119	    const caption = typeof (s as SlideIn).caption === "string" ? (s as SlideIn).caption!.slice(0, 300) : undefined;
> 120	    slides.push({ t_sec: t, caption });
> 121	  }
> 122	  return { slides };
> 123	}
> 124	
> 125	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
> 126	  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
> 127	  const json = (status: number, body: unknown) =>
> 128	    new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
> 129	
> 130	  try {
> 131	    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
> 132	    if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) return json(401, { error: "Unauthorized" });
> 133	
> 134	    const userClient = createClient(
> 135	      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
> 136	      (Deno.env.get("SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY"))!,
> 137	      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } },
> 138	    );
> 139	    const { data: { user } } = await userClient.auth.getUser();
> 140	    if (!user) return json(401, { error: "Unauthorized" });
> 141	
> 142	    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
> 143	    const sourceAssetId = typeof body.source_asset_id === "string" ? body.source_asset_id : "";
> 144	    if (!sourceAssetId) return json(400, { error: "source_asset_id_required" });
> 145	    const mode = body.mode === "carousel" || Array.isArray(body.slides) ? "carousel" : "repurpose";
> 146	
> 147	    let clips: ClipIn[] = [], slides: SlideIn[] = [];
> 148	    if (mode === "carousel") {
> 149	      const r = sanitizeSlides(body.slides);
> 150	      if (r.error) return json(422, { error: r.error, action: "Cada slide precisa de t_sec ≥ 0 (≤10 slides)." });
> 151	      slides = r.slides;
> 152	    } else {
> 153	      const r = sanitizeClips(body.clips);
> 154	      if (r.error) return json(422, { error: r.error, action: r.action ?? "Cada corte precisa de in_sec < out_sec (≤50 cortes)." });
> 155	      clips = r.clips;
> 156	    }
> 157	    const fps = Number.isFinite(body.fps) && body.fps >= 1 && body.fps <= 60 ? Math.round(body.fps) : 30;
> 158	    const handle = typeof body.handle === "string" ? body.handle.slice(0, 40) : undefined;
> 159	
> 160	    const admin = createClient(
> 161	      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
> 162	      (Deno.env.get("SB_SECRET_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!,
> 163	    );
> 164	
> 165	    // Resolve the source OWNER-SCOPED (server-trusted bucket/key; the .eq user_id is the tenant gate).
> 166	    const { data: asset } = await admin.from("creative_assets")
> 167	      .select("id, user_id, kind, storage_bucket, storage_key")
> 168	      .eq("id", sourceAssetId).eq("user_id", user.id).maybeSingle();
> 169	    if (!asset) return json(404, { error: "source_not_found", action: "O vídeo-fonte não existe ou não é seu." });
> 170	    if (asset.kind !== "video") return json(422, { error: "source_not_video" });
> 171	    const bucket = asset.storage_bucket as string, key = asset.storage_key as string;
> 172	    if (bucket === "local") {
> 173	      // Host-local master → ADMIN-ONLY (Sovereign directive 2026-07-12). The worker reads it from
> 174	      // repurpose-inbox/<uid>/ with a real-path containment guard; free-plan Supabase can't hold the master.
> 175	      const { data: adminRow } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("role").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("role", "admin").maybeSingle();
> 176	      if (!adminRow) return json(403, { error: "admin_only", action: "Repurpose de arquivo do host é restrito a administradores." });
> 177	    } else if (!ALLOWED_BUCKETS.has(bucket)) {
> 178	      return json(400, { error: "source_ref_invalid" });   // defense-in-depth; the worker re-checks (OTD-VR-006)
> 179	    }
> 180	    if (!key.startsWith(`${user.id}/`) || key.includes("..")) {
> 181	      return json(400, { error: "source_ref_invalid" });
> 182	    }
> 183	
> 184	    const composition = mode === "carousel"
> 185	      ? { mode, source: { bucket, key }, source_asset_id: asset.id, slides, handle }
> 186	      : { mode, source: { bucket, key }, source_asset_id: asset.id, clips, fps };
> 187	
> 188	    const { data: render, error: insErr } = await admin.from("video_renders").insert({
> 189	      user_id: user.id, engine: "repurpose", charged_mco: 0, composition,
> 190	    }).select("id").single();
> 191	    if (insErr || !render) return json(500, { error: "enqueue_failed", detail: insErr?.message });
> 192	
> 193	    return json(202, {
> 194	      ok: true, render_id: render.id, mode,
> 195	      count: mode === "carousel" ? slides.length : clips.length,
> 196	      transport: `poll://${render.id}`,
> 197	    });
> 198	  } catch (e) {
> 199	    console.error("video-repurpose-run error:", e);
> 200	    return json(500, { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error" });
> 201	  }
> 202	});
> 203	
> ```

> 179	async function drainOnce(): Promise<number> {
> 180	  const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - RUNNING_TIMEOUT_MS).toISOString();
> 181	  await supabase.from('video_renders').update({ state: 'queued' })
> 182	    .eq('engine', 'repurpose').eq('state', 'running').lt('updated_at', cutoff).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 183	
> 184	  const { data: queued } = await supabase.from('video_renders')
> 185	    .select('id').eq('engine', 'repurpose').eq('state', 'queued').order('created_at', { ascending: true }).limit(5);
> 186	  if (!queued?.length) return 0;
> 187	  let n = 0;
> 188	  for (const q of queued) {
> 189	    const row = await claim(q.id);
> 190	    if (!row) continue;
> 191	    await processRepurpose(row as Parameters<typeof processRepurpose>[0]);
> 192	    n++;
> 193	  }
> 194	  return n;
> 195	}
> 196	
> 197	async function main() {
> 198	  if (process.argv.includes('--once')) {
> 199	    const n = await drainOnce();
> 200	    console.log(`drained ${n} repurpose render(s)`);
> 201	    process.exit(0);
> 202	  }
> 203	  console.log('🎬 video-repurpose-bridge running — polling video_renders (engine=repurpose, queued)...');
> 204	  await logHealth('healthy', 'worker_started');
> 205	   
> 206	  while (true) {
> 207	    try { await drainOnce(); } catch (e) {
> 208	      console.error('poll cycle error:', e);
> 209	      await logHealth('degraded', 'poll_error', { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
> 210	    }
> 211	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, POLL_MS));
> 212	  }
> 213	}
> 214	
> 215	main();
> 216	
> ```

> 49	// Upload a large master directly to the private video bucket via a one-time signed URL (bypasses the client
> 50	// INSERT-policy gap; the URL authorizes only the owner-scoped key the server minted).
> 51	export function useMasterUpload() {
> 52	  return useMutation<UploadedMaster, Error, File>({
> 53	    mutationFn: async (file: File) => {
> 54	      const { data: sign, error: signErr } = await supabase.functions.invoke<SignUploadResp>(
> 55	        'ingest-external-asset',
> 56	        { body: { action: 'sign_upload', filename: file.name } },
> 57	      );
> 58	      if (signErr) throw new Error(await edgeErrorMessage(signErr, 'Falha ao preparar o upload.'));
> 59	      if (!sign?.storage_key || !sign.token) throw new Error('Resposta de upload inválida do servidor.');
> 60	
> 61	      const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
> 62	        .from(sign.bucket)
> 63	        .uploadToSignedUrl(sign.storage_key, sign.token, file, {
> 64	          contentType: file.type || 'video/mp4',
> 65	        });
> 66	      if (upErr) {
> 67	        // The most common real-world failure is the project-wide upload size limit — surface it actionably.
> 68	        throw new Error(
> 69	          `${upErr.message}. Se o arquivo for grande, aumente o limite de upload em Supabase → Storage → Settings.`,
> 70	        );
> 71	      }
> 72	      return {
> 73	        storage_bucket: sign.bucket,
> 74	        storage_key: sign.storage_key,
> 75	        fileName: file.name,
> 76	        sizeBytes: file.size,
> 77	      };
> 78	    },
> 79	  });
> 80	}
> 81	
> 82	// Drag-and-drop upload of a LARGE master straight to the host disk (repurpose-inbox/<uid>/) via the
> 83	// nginx-fronted host-upload service — the free-plan Supabase 50MB cap makes Storage impossible for a full
> 84	// documentary. XHR (not fetch) so we get upload progress. The Supabase JWT authenticates + admin-gates server-side.
> 85	// Cloudflare (free plan) caps each request body at 100MB → slice the master into <100MB chunks; each passes CF
> 86	// and the host appends them (renaming on the last chunk). Small files go as a single request. XHR gives progress.
> 87	const CHUNK_BYTES = 80 * 1024 * 1024;
> 88	
> 89	interface UploadResp { ok?: boolean; done?: boolean; filename?: string; bytes?: number }
> 90	
> 91	function xhrPost(body: Blob, headers: Record<string, string>, onLoaded: (loaded: number) => void): Promise<UploadResp> {
> 92	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
> 93	    const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
> 94	    xhr.open('POST', '/api/host-upload');
> 95	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headers)) xhr.setRequestHeader(k, v);
> 96	    xhr.upload.onprogress = (e) => { if (e.lengthComputable) onLoaded(e.loaded); };
> 97	    xhr.onload = () => {
> 98	      if (xhr.status === 200) {
> 99	        try { resolve(JSON.parse(xhr.responseText)); } catch { reject(new Error('Resposta inválida do servidor.')); }
> 100	      } else {
> 101	        let msg = `Falha no upload (HTTP ${xhr.status}).`;
> 102	        try {
> 103	          const b = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
> 104	          if (b.error === 'admin_only') msg = 'Upload restrito a administradores.';
> 105	          else if (b.error === 'unauthorized') msg = 'Sessão inválida — faça login novamente.';
> 106	          else if (b.error) msg = String(b.error);
> 107	        } catch { /* keep default */ }
> 108	        reject(new Error(msg));
> 109	      }
> 110	    };
> 111	    xhr.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Erro de rede no upload (endpoint /api/host-upload ativo?).'));
> 112	    xhr.send(body);
> 113	  });
> 114	}
> 115	
> 116	export async function uploadMasterToHost(
> 117	  file: File,
> 118	  onProgress: (pct: number) => void,
> 119	): Promise<{ filename: string; bytes: number }> {
> 120	  const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
> 121	  const token = session?.access_token;
> 122	  if (!token) throw new Error('Sessão expirada — faça login novamente.');
> 123	  // Headers must be ASCII — encode spaces/accents in the filename (server decodeURIComponent + sanitize).
> 124	  // X-Total-Bytes deixa o servidor VERIFICAR a integridade do arquivo montado antes de aceitá-lo.
> 125	  // Sem isso, um chunk perdido/duplicado produz um arquivo com tamanho plausível e mídia corrompida
> 126	  // (witnessed 2026-07-20: master do EP02 decodificava até ~2min e virava lixo depois).
> 127	  const base: Record<string, string> = {
> 128	    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
> 129	    'X-Filename': encodeURIComponent(file.name),
> 130	    'X-Total-Bytes': String(file.size),
> 131	  };
> 132	  const total = file.size;
> 133	  onProgress(0);
> 134	
> 135	  if (total <= CHUNK_BYTES) {
> 136	    const r = await xhrPost(file, base, (loaded) => onProgress(Math.round((loaded / total) * 100)));
> 137	    return { filename: r.filename ?? file.name, bytes: r.bytes ?? total };
> 138	  }
> 139	
> 140	  const uploadId = crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : `${Date.now()}-${Math.round(Math.random() * 1e9)}`;
> 141	  const count = Math.ceil(total / CHUNK_BYTES);
> 142	  let uploaded = 0;
> 143	  let result: UploadResp = {};
> 144	  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
> 145	    const blob = file.slice(i * CHUNK_BYTES, Math.min((i + 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, total));
> 146	    result = await xhrPost(
> 147	      blob,
> 148	      { ...base, 'X-Upload-Id': uploadId, 'X-Chunk-Index': String(i), 'X-Chunk-Count': String(count) },
> 149	      (loaded) => onProgress(Math.round(((uploaded + loaded) / total) * 100)),
> 150	    );
> 151	    uploaded += blob.size;
> 152	    onProgress(Math.round((uploaded / total) * 100));
> 153	  }
> 154	  return { filename: result.filename ?? file.name, bytes: result.bytes ?? total };
> 155	}

> 228	// Masters JÁ ingeridos (RLS own) — evita re-subir 500MB+ quando o vídeo já está no acervo.
> 229	// Inclui o estado do SRT porque é ele que habilita o gerador de cortes por IA, e um aviso de
> 230	// chave inconsistente: o host sanitiza o nome ao gravar, então um storage_key com espaço/parêntese
> 231	// aponta para um arquivo que não existe (causa real de `source_not_found`).
> 232	// Veredito de integridade gravado por GET /api/host-probe em creative_assets.metadata.health.
> 233	// SOP: docs/processes/repurpose-host-infra-provisioning.md §"Rota de saúde /api/host-probe".
> 234	export type MasterHealthStatus = 'ok' | 'corrupt' | 'missing';
> 235	export interface MasterHealth {
> 236	  status: MasterHealthStatus;
> 237	  bytes: number | null;
> 238	  duration_seconds: number | null;
> 239	  checked_at: string;
> 240	  spots?: { at_sec: number; exit: number; frames: number; clean: boolean }[];
> 241	  detail?: string;
> 242	}
> 243	
> 244	// Selo exibido na UI. `unverified` NÃO é um veredito do servidor — é a ausência honesta de um
> 245	// (nunca sondado, sondagem velha, tamanho divergente, ou bucket sem host para sondar).
> 246	export type MasterHealthSeal = 'ok' | 'corrupt' | 'missing' | 'unverified';
> 247	
> 248	/** Um veredito verde vale 7 dias; depois disso o arquivo pode ter mudado sob nossos pés. */
> 249	export const HEALTH_FRESH_MS = 7 * 86_400_000;
> 250	
> 251	/**
> 252	 * Rebaixa o veredito do servidor a `unverified` sempre que ele não puder ser afirmado com honestidade:
> 253	 *  - master fora de `bucket='local'` → não há host para sondar (dizer "íntegro" seria fabricar prova, Lei 1);
> 254	 *  - nunca sondado;
> 255	 *  - `checked_at` com mais de 7 dias;
> 256	 *  - `health.bytes` divergente de `file_size_bytes` (quando ambos existirem) → o arquivo mudou desde a sondagem.
> 257	 * `corrupt`/`missing` NUNCA são suavizados — um veredito ruim vencido continua ruim até ser refutado.
> 258	 */
> 259	export function sealMasterHealth(
> 260	  health: MasterHealth | null,
> 261	  bucket: string,
> 262	  sizeBytes: number | null,
> 263	  now = Date.now(),
> 264	): MasterHealthSeal {
> 265	  if (bucket !== 'local') return 'unverified';
> 266	  if (!health?.status) return 'unverified';
> 267	  if (health.status !== 'ok') return health.status;
> 268	  const checked = Date.parse(health.checked_at ?? '');
> 269	  if (!Number.isFinite(checked) || now - checked > HEALTH_FRESH_MS) return 'unverified';
> 270	  if (sizeBytes != null && health.bytes != null && sizeBytes !== health.bytes) return 'unverified';
> 271	  return 'ok';
> 272	}

> 274	export interface IngestedMaster {
> 275	  id: string; title: string; storageKey: string; bucket: string;
> 276	  hasSrt: boolean; createdAt: string; keyLooksSanitized: boolean;
> 277	  sizeBytes: number | null; durationSeconds: number | null;

> 281	export function useIngestedMasters() {
> 282	  const query = useQuery({
> 283	    queryKey: ['ingested_masters'],
> 284	    queryFn: async (): Promise<IngestedMaster[]> => {
> 285	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 286	        .from('creative_assets')
> 287	        .select('id, title, storage_key, storage_bucket, metadata, created_at, file_size_bytes, duration_seconds')
> 288	        .eq('kind', 'video')
> 289	        .eq('source_module', 'external')
> 290	        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
> 291	        .limit(30);
> 292	      if (error) throw error;
> 293	      return (data ?? []).map((r) => {
> 294	        const meta = r.metadata as { srt?: { pt?: string; en?: string }; health?: MasterHealth } | null;
> 295	        const srt = meta?.srt;
> 296	        const file = (r.storage_key ?? '').split('/').pop() ?? '';
> 297	        const sizeBytes = r.file_size_bytes == null ? null : Number(r.file_size_bytes);
> 298	        const health = meta?.health ?? null;
> 299	        return {
> 300	          id: r.id,
> 301	          title: r.title ?? file,
> 302	          storageKey: r.storage_key,
> 303	          bucket: r.storage_bucket,
> 304	          hasSrt: ((srt?.pt ?? srt?.en ?? '').length) > 100,
> 305	          createdAt: r.created_at,
> 306	          // O host grava só [a-zA-Z0-9._-]; qualquer outro caractere na chave = arquivo inexistente.
> 307	          keyLooksSanitized: /^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$/.test(file),
> 308	          sizeBytes,
> 309	          durationSeconds: r.duration_seconds == null ? null : Number(r.duration_seconds),
> 310	          health,
> 311	          seal: sealMasterHealth(health, r.storage_bucket, sizeBytes),
> 312	        };
> 313	      });
> 314	    },
> 315	    staleTime: 30_000,
> 316	  });
> 317	  return { ...query, masters: query.data ?? [] };
> 318	}
> 319	
> 320	// Dispara a sondagem de integridade no HOST (o único lugar onde a verdade existe: o arquivo em disco).
> 321	// Não dá para saber se um master é decodificável a partir do banco — `file_size_bytes` é NULL nas linhas
> 322	// legadas e, mesmo preenchido, TAMANHO NÃO PROVA DECODABILIDADE (chunks reordenados dão tamanho idêntico).
> 323	export function useProbeMasterHealth() {
> 324	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 325	  return useMutation<MasterHealth, Error, { storageKey: string }>({
> 326	    mutationFn: async ({ storageKey }) => {
> 327	      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
> 328	      const token = session?.access_token;
> 329	      if (!token) throw new Error('Sessão expirada — entre novamente.');
> 330	      const res = await fetch(`/api/host-probe?key=${encodeURIComponent(storageKey)}`, {
> 331	        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
> 332	      });
> 333	      const body = await res.json().catch(() => null) as { error?: string; status?: string } | null;
> 334	      // 404 = veredito `missing` (o arquivo sumiu do disco) — é resposta legítima, não erro de transporte.
> 335	      if (!res.ok && res.status !== 404) {
> 336	        throw new Error(
> 337	          body?.error === 'admin_only' ? 'Verificação restrita a administradores.'
> 338	            : body?.error === 'unauthorized' ? 'Sessão inválida — entre novamente.'
> 339	            : body?.error ? String(body.error)
> 340	            : `Falha ao verificar o arquivo (HTTP ${res.status}).`,
> 341	        );
> 342	      }
> 343	      // ⚠️ Um veredito só é veredito se tiver a forma de um. Sem esta checagem, QUALQUER 200 não-JSON vira
> 344	      // `{}` → `status` undefined → a UI acusava "arquivo corrompido" sobre um master saudável. É o que
> 345	      // acontece HOJE em produção: `/api/host-probe` não está no nginx vivo (só host-upload e host-media),
> 346	      // então o `location /` devolve o index.html da SPA com HTTP 200 (medido 2026-07-20: CT=text/html,
> 347	      // 3649 bytes). Condenar um master de 1,3 GB por causa de uma rota ausente é o inverso exato do bug
> 348	      // que esta feature existe para corrigir — falhe alto, e diga a causa provável.
> 349	      if (!body || (body.status !== 'ok' && body.status !== 'corrupt' && body.status !== 'missing')) {
> 350	        throw new Error(
> 351	          'A verificação de integridade não está disponível (a rota /api/host-probe não respondeu um veredito). '
> 352	          + 'Nenhuma conclusão foi tirada sobre este arquivo.',
> 353	        );
> 354	      }
> 355	      return body as unknown as MasterHealth;
> 356	    },
> 357	    onSuccess: () => {
> 358	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['ingested_masters'] });
> 359	    },
> 360	  });
> 361	}

> 363	// Excluir um asset do acervo. A policy RLS `creative_assets_delete_own` (auth.uid() = user_id) já existia —
> 364	// só faltava a UI expor. NOTA HONESTA: isto remove o REGISTRO; o arquivo em disco/bucket permanece (um
> 365	// re-upload com o mesmo nome o sobrescreve). Limpeza de objeto órfão é rotina separada.
> 366	export function useDeleteAsset() {
> 367	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 368	  return useMutation<void, Error, string>({
> 369	    mutationFn: async (assetId: string) => {
> 370	      // `.select()` returns the rows RLS actually let us delete. Without it a blocked delete resolves
> 371	      // with error=null and 0 rows, and the caller fires a success toast for a row that still exists —
> 372	      // the SELECT policy is `own OR org member` while the DELETE policy is owner-only, so an asset
> 373	      // shared into the org renders a delete button that silently no-ops. Fail loud instead (Lei 1).
> 374	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 375	        .from('creative_assets')
> 376	        .delete()
> 377	        .eq('id', assetId)
> 378	        .select('id');
> 379	      if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
> 380	      if (!data || data.length === 0) {
> 381	        throw new Error('Você não tem permissão para excluir este asset — ele pertence a outra conta.');
> 382	      }
> 383	    },
> 384	    onSuccess: () => {
> 385	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['ingested_masters'] });
> 386	      // ⚠️ 'creative-assets' com HÍFEN — é a chave real da galeria (useCreativeAssets.ts:15,
> 387	      // ["creative-assets", kind ?? "all"]). A variante com underscore NUNCA casava, então o grid
> 388	      // de /dashboard/spaces/assets não atualizava após excluir e o botão parecia quebrado.
> 389	      // O prefix-match do TanStack cobre todas as variantes de `kind` de uma vez.
> 390	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['creative-assets'] });
> 391	    },
> 392	  });
> 393	}

> 462	  });

The UI needs to set both caption_mode='beats' and caption_style='motion-graphics-hero-9x16' together. I should verify that the UI components already iterate over CAPTION_STYLE_OPTIONS and automatically stamp the new option onto clips without requiring edits to those components.

## 🤖 Claude *(19:20:01)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I've got Playwright and ffmpeg available, so I can create a proof of concept. I'll write a test harness that generates a short synthetic video with some motion to give the saliency detector something to work with, then verify that the zone stamping actually reaches the template and that the segment-core properly calls pickCalmestZone.

The strongest proof would be to run the full end-to-end flow through segment-core itself: create a synthetic master video, call segmentVideo with motion-graphics-hero-9x16 caption style and text beats, then inspect the output to confirm the card gets composited at a zone chosen by saliency rather than a default position. So the center-crop to 9:16 portrait mode only preserves the central vertical strip of the original 16:9 frame, which means I need to position the calm and busy regions strategically within that central area—left side flat, right side busy—so that after cropping, saliency will detect the left zone as the calm region to place the caption.

The real proof I need isn't nailing down the exact saliency calculation, but rather demonstrating that the motion-graphics card renders and composites correctly when the style is set to motion-graphics-hero-9x16, anchored in a detected zone rather than locked to the footer. Even if saliency defaults to lower-left, seeing the card appear as a two-level kicker-plus-hero in a corner (not a footer bar) confirms the wiring works and the style is functionally distinct from the karaoke family.

I should design the master frame with a clear busy-right and calm-left split so the saliency pick lands in a left zone that's visibly not the default-right placement, which would give stronger proof of the system working as intended.

Actually, since the default is already lower-left, I need to flip the strategy—make the bottom half busy with high-frequency noise and the top half calm flat gray. After the center-crop to 9:16, saliency should sample the upper zones as calmest and pick something like upper-left or upper-right, which would visibly differ from the lower-left default and prove saliency is actively choosing based on content. NOT a repo file \u2014 scratchpad only.\n *\n * Proves end-to-end that the repurpose rail, when caption_style='motion-graphics-hero-9x16':\n *   1) resolves the style in segment-core,\n *   2) calls pickCalmestZone (saliency) for THIS style only,\n *   3) stamps the picked zone onto every beat,\n *   4) renders the motion-graphics template and composites it over the reframed footage.\n *\n * The synthetic master is CALM on top / BUSY on bottom \u2192 saliency MUST pick an UPPER zone,\n * which is visibly different from the lower-left default. So a card in the upper band = the\n * saliency actually drove placement through my wiring (not a hardcoded default).\n */\nimport { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { segmentVideo } from '/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/video-repurpose/segment-core.ts';\nimport { pickCalmestZone } from '/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/video-repurpose/zone-saliency.ts';\n\nconst BASE = '/tmp/claude-1001/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/d0ed63c2-9678-4c01-95fc-6d554d6141c3/scratchpad/mg-proof';\nmkdirSync(BASE, { recursive: true });\nconst master = `${BASE}/master.mp4`;\nconst outDir = `${BASE}/out`;\n\n// Master: 1280x720 (16:9), 4s @ 15fps. geq \u2192 bottom half (Y>=360) is per-pixel random noise (BUSY, max edges),\n// top half flat gray 128 (CALM). After center-crop to 9:16 the full height is kept, so top stays calm / bottom busy.\nconsole.log('\u2192 generating synthetic master (calm top / busy bottom)\u2026');\nconst gen = spawnSync('ffmpeg', [\n  '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error',\n  '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'color=c=black:s=1280x720:r=15:d=4',\n  '-vf', \"geq=lum='if(gte(Y,360),random(1)*255,128)':cb=128:cr=128\",\n  '-c:v', 'libx264', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', master,\n], { encoding: 'utf8' });\nif (gen.status !== 0) { console.error('master gen failed:', gen.stderr); process.exit(1); }\n\n// Independent read of what saliency picks for the window [0,4] (logged for the record).\nconst pick = await pickCalmestZone(master, 0, 4);\nconsole.log(`\u2192 saliency pick: zone=${pick.zone} degraded=${pick.degraded} elapsedMs=${pick.elapsedMs.toFixed(0)}`);\nconsole.log('  ranked:', pick.scores.map((s) => `${s.zone}:${s.agitation === Infinity ? 'inf' : s.agitation.toFixed(2)}`).join('  '));\n\n// Full rail through MY wiring: caption_mode='beats' + caption_style='motion-graphics-hero-9x16'.\nconsole.log('\u2192 segmentVideo (motion-graphics-hero-9x16)\u2026');\nconst clips = await segmentVideo({\n  sourcePath: master,\n  outDir,\n  fps: 15,\n  clips: [{\n    in_sec: 0, out_sec: 4, reframe: '9:16',\n    caption_mode: 'beats',\n    caption_style: 'motion-graphics-hero-9x16',\n    text_beats: [{ t_start: 0.3, t_end: 3.6, phrase: 'ISSO MUDA TUDO', emphasis: 'hook' }],\n  }],\n});\nconsole.log('\u2192 clip:', JSON.stringify(clips[0]));\n\n// Extract a frame at t=1.8s (mid-beat) \u2192 PNG for ocular Read.\nconst framePng = `${BASE}/frame.png`;\nconst fr = spawnSync('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error', '-ss', '1.8', '-i', clips[0].path, '-frames:v', '1', framePng], { encoding: 'utf8' });\nif (fr.status !== 0) { console.error('frame extract failed:', fr.stderr); process.exit(1); }\nconsole.log(`\u2192 frame written: ${framePng}`);\n"
